# Imported Westwood libraries

Generated by `tools/vc6/importer.py report` from `docs/data/imports.json`. Each library was imported from EA's published copies (R Renegade, G Generals, Z Zero Hour; commits Renegade `CnC_Renegade@3e00c3a`, Generals `CnC_Generals_Zero_Hour@0a05454`, Zero Hour `CnC_Generals_Zero_Hour@0a05454`), file by file, keeping the copy whose compiled code matches client.exe best, then splicing in whole function definitions from other copies where that adds byte-exact functions without losing any. "In tree": client functions our own source reproduces byte for byte (compiled with VC6 SP4, `/MT /W3 /O2 /Ob2`). See docs/library-matching.md Â§3.5.

## Summary

"Exact, ambiguous address" (D54): functions whose compiled bytes occur at several places in client.exe, all copies identical (relocated fields included), so no single address can be credited; counted once per client function, never as pairs.

| Library | Base copy | Source files | From another copy | Functions merged | In tree | Exact, ambiguous address | Best single copy |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| app | reconstructed | 155 | 0 | 0 | 1997 | 97 | - |
| binkmovie | Renegade | 4 | 0 | 0 | 22 | 9 | 21 |
| wlib | Zero Hour | 7 | 0 | 0 | 78 | 7 | 74 |
| wnet | Zero Hour | 4 | 3 | 0 | 41 | 4 | 7 |
| ww3d2 | Renegade | 108 | 32 | 11 | 2707 | 157 | 1323 |
| wwaudio | Renegade | 19 | 0 | 0 | 410 | 31 | 205 |
| wwdebug | Zero Hour | 3 | 0 | 0 | 88 | 16 | 65 |
| wwlib | Renegade | 73 | 3 | 1 | 632 | 51 | 535 |
| wwmath | Renegade | 35 | 2 | 0 | 422 | 39 | 366 |
| wwsaveload | Zero Hour | 12 | 0 | 0 | 87 | 34 | 75 |
| wwshade | Zero Hour | 22 | 0 | 0 | 249 | 8 | 62 |

## Local changes

* `wwdebug/wwdebug.h`: Zero Hour's wwdebug.h includes Generals' GameEngine debug.h; gamedebug.h gives the release build's definitions of the macros it uses (all compile to nothing).
* `ww3d2/meshbuild.h`: MeshBuilderClass::MAX_STAGES is 8 in E&B, as in Zero Hour (Renegade and Generals: 2). Found from VertexMaterialClass: E&B's CRCDirty is at +0x6C, 0x30 bytes later than with 2 stages (Mapper[] and UVSource[] are sized by it).
* `wwmath/matrix3d.h`: Zero Hour's Matrix3D helpers (preMul, postMul, mul, mulVector3, mulVector3Array), without Zero Hour's ALLOW_TEMPORARIES rework of the operators (E&B's operator code is Renegade's). Zero Hour's streak.cpp, whose functions match the client, uses them.
* `wwmath/matrix3d.h`: their definitions, verbatim from Zero Hour.
* `wwlib/sharebuf.h`: ShareBufferClass also has Zero Hour's constructor taking a memory-tag argument, ignored in a release build (Zero Hour only stores it in debug builds), so Zero Hour code that passes it compiles beside Renegade code that doesn't. A separate overload, not a default argument: a default would add a push to every existing call. Renegade's class otherwise (no W3DMPO base).
* `wwlib/sharebuf.h`: its definition (the same body as the one-argument constructor).
* `ww3d2/w3d_file.h`: W3dEmitterExtraInfoStruct (Zero Hour's) keeps its 40 bytes, but E&B uses the byte after FutureStartTime as a flag (set by ParticleEmitterDefClass's extra virtual, vtable slot 19 at 0x914520; passed to every emitter the definition creates); named UnknownFlag.
* `ww3d2/part_ldr.h`: ParticleEmitterDefClass has an extra virtual after Set_Future_Start_Time (the client's vtable at 0xB10920, slot 19) setting the flag: named Set_Unknown_Flag. A bool argument stored as `? 1 : 0` into a uint8 reproduces the client's test/setne.
* `ww3d2/part_ldr.h`: and a getter (ParticleEmitterClass::Create_From_Definition reads the byte and tests it).
* `ww3d2/scene.h`: SceneClass has Zero Hour's scene IDs: Get_Scene_ID() const (returns SCENE_ID_SCENE) right after the destructor, as in the client's vtable (0xB120FC, slot 2). Zero Hour's virtual Render is not in the client.
* `ww3d2/scene.h`: SimpleSceneClass::Get_Scene_ID() (not const, so a new virtual, not an override: as in Zero Hour) returns SCENE_ID_SIMPLE (the client's slot 23 returns 1).
* `wwaudio/audiblesound.h`: AudibleSoundClass has two E&B ints after m_LoopsLeft (+0x7C, +0x80; every later member is 8 bytes later in the client), set to 4 and -1 by both constructors (client 0x9FCA50, 0x9FCE60) and not copied by operator=. Meaning not known: named m_Unknown7C, m_Unknown80.
* `wwaudio/audiblesound.h`: AudibleSoundClass::Update_Volume 0x9F7910 (vtable slot 54) passes +0x6C (m_RealVolume) to Set_Volume.
* `wwaudio/audiblesound.cpp`: both constructors initialise them.
* `wwaudio/audiblesound.cpp`: AudibleSoundClass::Play 0x9FD3E0: while WWAudioClass +0x19A5 is set (0x9FD419) and the type is not music (+0x48), adds the sound to the playlist as STATE_PAUSED (0x9FD430), resets the timestamp and loop count, zeroes the position and returns true.
* `wwaudio/audiblesound.cpp`: AudibleSoundClass::operator= 0x9FD090 copies +0x7C and +0x80 after m_LoopsLeft (0x9FD0C8..0x9FD0D4).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.h`: Sound3DClass has E&B fields: 12 bytes after m_CurrentVelocity (+0x148) and four ints after m_LastUpdate (+0x160 to +0x16C), all zeroed by the constructors (client 0x9F76A0, 0x9F7C90, 0x9F9220) and not copied by operator=. Meaning not known: m_Unknown148 (a Vector3 by size), m_Unknown160 to m_Unknown16C.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.h`: the four ints at the end.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.h`: Sound3DClass::Set_Velocity 0x9F8920 takes a second float (ret 8; [ebp+0xC] read at 0x9F8C69, scaled by 0.05/33.3), and Sound3DClass::On_Frame_Update 0x9F7E80 passes the elapsed time (call [vtbl+0x184] with two arguments). So the one-argument Set_Velocity of SoundPseudo3DClass and Listener3DClass no longer override it: SoundPseudo3DClass's vtable 0xB15EE4 keeps 0x9F8920 in slot 97 and has the empty one-argument virtual (0x9FFA90, ret 4) as its first own slot (105); Listener3DClass's vtable 0xB163D0 has it (0xA02DE0, ret 4) as its last slot (121), after Set_Sample_Playback_Rate.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.h`: Sound3DClass::_UnknownDopplerFlag (name not known, D10): the byte at 0xC404F8, tested by On_Frame_Update (0x9F7EFD) and Set_Listener_Transform (0x9F84C5), written by the app from 'Doppler_Enabled' (0x51461C).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.h`: Sound3DClass::Set_Listener_Transform 0x9F84A0 stores the listener translation change per ms in +0x148; Set_Velocity 0x9F8920 subtracts it.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.h`: (comment only: Set_Listener_Transform 0x9F84A0 times the listener velocity with +0x160).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.h`: Sound3DClass +0x164, +0x168, +0x16C are floats: Set_Velocity 0x9F8920 compares the pitch with +0x164 and +0x168 (fcomp at 0x9F8CAD, 0x9F8CC8) and multiplies the velocity by +0x16C (fld at 0x9F8BC5).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: both constructors zero m_Unknown148.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: and m_Unknown160 to m_Unknown16C.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::Update_Miles_Transform 0x9F8680 takes the Miles lock (AIL_lock 0x9F8686, AIL_unlock 0x9F874A).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::Set_DropOff_Radius 0x9F8D00 takes the Miles lock (AIL_lock 0x9F8D03).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::Set_Max_Vol_Radius 0x9F8D70 takes the Miles lock.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::Update_Edge_Volume 0x9F8180 restores the full volume inside the falloff start (Internal_Set_Volume(m_RealVolume) at 0x9F8337).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: (the definition: the same).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: (its definition).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::On_Frame_Update 0x9F7E80: E&B takes the Miles lock, re-enables the auto-velocity block (Renegade's '#if 0'), gated by the flag at 0xC404F8 (0x9F7EFD), and passes the elapsed time to Set_Velocity (call [vtbl+0x184], two arguments); then Update_Miles_Transform (0x9F8680) always, after Update_Edge_Volume.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::Set_Listener_Transform 0x9F84A0: when the Doppler flag (0xC404F8) and auto-calc are set, E&B stores the listener's velocity per ms in m_Unknown148 (0x9F8519..0x9F852D), timed with m_Unknown160 (at least 1 ms; 0x9F856C..0x9F85D9); no Update_Miles_Transform (On_Frame_Update calls it now). The != keeps operator== out of line (0x9F8630, called at 0x9F84B4).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: (Set_Velocity's constants, defined before it).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::Set_Velocity 0x9F8920: E&B's Doppler effect. The velocity relative to the listener (minus m_Unknown148) is normalized and dotted with the direction to the listener; the relative velocity scaled by that is smoothed against the previous one (0.85, 0xB15864), scaled by m_Unknown16C; its length (at most 3.4, 0xB15A3C) gives the pitch 3.4/(3.4 -+ speed), moved at most elapsed*0.05/33.3 (0xB15A38) from Get_Pitch_Factor (vtbl+0x104) and clamped to [m_Unknown164, m_Unknown168] before Set_Pitch_Factor (vtbl+0x108). The three constants are named (not folded: 1.0-0.85 is computed at 0x9F8B52); their names are not known.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::Set_Transform 0x9F8350: E&B no longer copies m_Transform to m_PrevTransform (On_Frame_Update does) nor calls Update_Miles_Transform (On_Frame_Update does): one Matrix3D copy before Set_Dirty (0x9F8412), and the function ends after the initialisation test.
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::Set_Position 0x9F8760 takes the Miles lock (AIL_lock 0x9F8766, AIL_unlock 0x9F890E).
* `wwaudio/sound3d.cpp`: Sound3DClass::Set_Position 0x9F8760 no longer passes the position to Miles (On_Frame_Update's Update_Miles_Transform does): nothing after the initialisation test but the unlock.
* `wwmath/matrix4.h`: Matrix4x4 (Zero Hour's name for Matrix4) as an alias, for the Zero Hour code imported beside Renegade's WWMath; E&B's own name for the class is not known.
* `wwmath/matrix4.h`: Zero Hour's 16-float Matrix4 constructor and Init (NH, 11/06/2001: older than E&B's WW3D2), used by Zero Hour's mapper.cpp, whose texture-matrix mappers the client has (ScaleTextureMapperClass::Apply 0x9322F0 calls Calculate_Texture_Matrix, vtable slot 8, then sets D3DTS_TEXTURE0+Stage).
* `wwmath/matrix4.h`: Zero Hour's 16-float Matrix4 constructor and Init (NH, 11/06/2001: older than E&B's WW3D2), used by Zero Hour's mapper.cpp, whose texture-matrix mappers the client has (ScaleTextureMapperClass::Apply 0x9322F0 calls Calculate_Texture_Matrix, vtable slot 8, then sets D3DTS_TEXTURE0+Stage).
* `wwmath/matrix4.h`: Zero Hour's 16-float Matrix4 constructor and Init (NH, 11/06/2001: older than E&B's WW3D2), used by Zero Hour's mapper.cpp, whose texture-matrix mappers the client has (ScaleTextureMapperClass::Apply 0x9322F0 calls Calculate_Texture_Matrix, vtable slot 8, then sets D3DTS_TEXTURE0+Stage).
* `wwmath/matrix4.h`: Zero Hour's 16-float Matrix4 constructor and Init (NH, 11/06/2001: older than E&B's WW3D2), used by Zero Hour's mapper.cpp, whose texture-matrix mappers the client has (ScaleTextureMapperClass::Apply 0x9322F0 calls Calculate_Texture_Matrix, vtable slot 8, then sets D3DTS_TEXTURE0+Stage).
* `wwmath/matrix3.h`: Matrix3x3 (Zero Hour's name for Matrix3) as an alias, for the Zero Hour code imported beside Renegade's WWMath.
* `ww3d2/part_buf.h`: ParticleBufferClass has Zero Hour's line-group members: the GroupID buffer pointer after APT (every member from PingPongPosition, +0x204, is 4 bytes later in the client) and CurrentGroupID at the end; not Zero Hour's FutureStartTime (members before +0x204 don't move).
* `ww3d2/part_buf.h`: CurrentGroupID at the end.
* `ww3d2/part_buf.h`: NewParticleStruct has Zero Hour's GroupID (E&B's emitter, Zero Hour's code, sets it).
* `ww3d2/part_buf.h`: Zero Hour's Set_Current_GroupID.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.h`: ParticleEmitterClass (Zero Hour's) takes one more constructor argument, a bool after line_props (the client's constructor, 0x97D180, ends `ret 0x78`), kept in UnknownFlag.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.h`: E&B keeps the future start time in the emitter (ms), not in the buffer as Zero Hour does.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.h`: FutureStartTime (+0xF4): the constructor stores future_start * 1000 there; Reset() starts EmitRemain from it.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.h`: UnknownFlag (+0xFD), copied by the copy constructor.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.h`: ParticleEmitterClass: E&B's emitter-space emission (names not known, D10): Create_New_Particles and Initialize_Particle without the emitter transform (0x97EB80, 0x97ECB0), used by Emit when UnknownFlag is set.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.h`: ParticleEmitterClass::Unknown_Get_Position (E&B, name not known, D10): called by ParticleBufferClass::Render_Line (0x95E290).
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: the extra constructor argument.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: the constructor initialises FutureStartTime and UnknownFlag.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: the copy constructor copies them.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: the buffer is not given the future start time (E&B's buffer has no FutureStartTime).
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: Reset() starts EmitRemain from FutureStartTime (the client loads +0xF4 there).
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: Renegade's texture API (MIP_LEVELS_ALL is TextureClass's).
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: Create_From_Definition passes the definition's flag.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: WW3D2 is built with ENABLE_WWPROFILE, but E&B's ParticleEmitterClass::On_Frame_Update 0x97DEF0 has no sampler (no EH frame; it starts with the Active test).
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: ParticleEmitterClass as E&B has it: the constructor (0x97D180) keeps FutureStartTime at 0 unless future_start > 0 and starts EmitRemain from it after VelRand->Scale (0x97D368); Emit (0x97E330) emits in emitter space when UnknownFlag (+0xFD) is set (0x97EB80, 0x97ECB0; profile name 'ParticleEmitter::Emit'); Create_New_Particles (0x97E530) multiplies by 1/frametime and lerps the origin by value; Build_Definition (0x97EDE0) passes UnknownFlag on after the shader (slot 0x4C).
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: ParticleEmitterClass as E&B has it: the constructor (0x97D180) keeps FutureStartTime at 0 unless future_start > 0 and starts EmitRemain from it after VelRand->Scale (0x97D368); Emit (0x97E330) emits in emitter space when UnknownFlag (+0xFD) is set (0x97EB80, 0x97ECB0; profile name 'ParticleEmitter::Emit'); Create_New_Particles (0x97E530) multiplies by 1/frametime and lerps the origin by value; Build_Definition (0x97EDE0) passes UnknownFlag on after the shader (slot 0x4C).

* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: ParticleEmitterClass::Unknown_Get_Position (0x97E260, between Get_Velocity_Random and Set_Base_Velocity): the origin when UnknownFlag (+0xFD) is set, Get_Position() otherwise; nothing for a NULL pointer.
* `ww3d2/part_emt.cpp`: MipCountType and MIP_LEVELS_* are global in Zero Hour's texture API (ww3dformat.h), as in Zero Hour's copy of this file.
* `ww3d2/meshmdl.h`: MeshModelClass keeps the PolygonRendererList member Renegade's later revision removed (Generals and Zero Hour have it after MatInfo): GapFiller is 24 bytes (a MultiListClass) later in the client.
* `ww3d2/meshmdl.h`: Zero Hour's MeshModelClass::modify_for_overbright: 0x9BD3A0 turns primary gradient MODULATE (1) into 5 (and ah,0xF7; or ah,0x14) in the single shader and the shader arrays.
* `wwlib/random.h`: E&B's Pick_Random_Number works in unsigned arithmetic and guards the full-range mask: all four operator()(min, max) at 0x9D0000, 0x9D0180, 0x9D02B0, 0x9D0510 compare unsigned (jbe/ja) and reload an all-ones mask when the shift leaves none (variant A in .work/random).
* `wwlib/random.h`: the mask, unsigned, all ones when the range needs every bit.
* `wwlib/random.h`: and the pick, compared unsigned with the magnitude.
* `ww3d2/shader.h`: Zero Hour's SSCAT_SCREEN static sort category (src ONE, dest ONE_MINUS_SRC_COLOR): ShaderClass::Get_SS_Category (0x93CE00) and Guess_Sort_Level (0x93CE90) test for it.
* `ww3d2/shader.h`: E&B's ShaderClass has Zero Hour's detail color modes and GRADIENT_MODULATE2X (5) in place of Renegade's GRADIENT_DOTPRODUCT3: ShaderClass::Apply 0x93B510 switches detail color modes 9-12 and maps primary gradient 5 to D3DTOP_MODULATE/MODULATE2X by (TextureOpCaps & 5).
* `ww3d2/shader.h`: E&B's ShaderClass has Zero Hour's detail color modes and GRADIENT_MODULATE2X (5) in place of Renegade's GRADIENT_DOTPRODUCT3: ShaderClass::Apply 0x93B510 switches detail color modes 9-12 and maps primary gradient 5 to D3DTOP_MODULATE/MODULATE2X by (TextureOpCaps & 5).
* `ww3d2/rinfo.h`: RenderInfoClass has Zero Hour's Texture_Projector after light_environment (not its three alpha overrides): the client's constructor (0x930F80) zeroes one more member, and every member from +0x14 is 4 bytes later (SpecialRenderInfoClass's own from 0x124).
* `ww3d2/rinfo.h`: the member.
* `ww3d2/rinfo.cpp`: initialised to NULL, as in Zero Hour.
* `ww3d2/dx8caps.h`: DX8Caps has Zero Hour's twelve extra capability flags (SupportDepthStencilFormat[8] before SupportZBias; SupportModAlphaAddClr, SupportDot3, SupportPointSprites, SupportCubemaps after SupportAnisotropicFiltering): every member from VertexShaderVersion is 12 bytes later in the client, DeviceId too, so not Zero Hour's MaxSimultaneousTextures. Accessors as Zero Hour's.
* `ww3d2/dx8caps.h`: the four flags after SupportAnisotropicFiltering.
* `ww3d2/dx8caps.h`: Zero Hour's accessors for them.
* `ww3d2/dx8caps.h`: and Zero Hour's Get_Pixel_Shader_Major_Version (Renegade's is misspelt Majon) and Support_Depth_Stencil_Format.
* `ww3d2/dx8caps.h`: Zero Hour's Get_Pixel_Shader_Minor_Version reads PixelShaderVersion (Renegade's reads the vertex shader's): ShdBumpDiffDefClass::Init 0xA0B9B0 and ShdBumpSpecDefClass::Init take major and minor from the same field, +0x12C.
* `ww3d2/dx8caps.h`: E&B knows Zero Hour's Matrox Parhelia devices: the client's Matrox name table (0xBC4BE0) ends with 'Parhelia AGP 8X' (0xBC4C94), and Get_Matrox_Device 0x9AFDC0 maps 0x0527/0x0528.
* `ww3d2/dx8caps.h`: E&B's Compute_Caps has Zero Hour's point-sprite, ModAlphaAddClr, Dot3 and cubemap flags with their logs ('ModAlphaAddClr support: %s' 0xBC5294, 'Dot3 support: %s' 0xBC5280) and calls Check_Depth_Stencil_Support 0x9B1C20 ('Supports depth stencil format: %s' 0xBC54FC) after Check_Render_To_Texture_Support; there is no Check_Maximum_Texture_Support call (0x9B0160).
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: E&B's class IDs end as Zero Hour's: ShdMeshClass::Class_ID (0xA0A7E0, in the table with ShdMeshClass::Render) returns 0x24, CLASSID_LAND + 1, where Renegade has CLASSID_RENEGADE_TERRAIN (unused in the libraries).
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: RenderObjClass has Zero Hour's render hook (RenderHookClass, the RenderHook member at the end of the class, Get/Set_Render_Hook, deleted in the destructor) but not Zero Hour's ObjectScale/ObjectColor. Found from the +4 shift of every member after 0x88 in HLodClass, SphereRenderObjClass, RingRenderObjClass, ... (tools/re/layoutdiff.py).
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: the destructor deletes the render hook.
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: render hook accessors.
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: the render hook member.
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: RenderObjClass's vtable as in the client (113 slots, at 0xAE84F8; tools/re/vtables.py): Zero Hour's Remove_Sub_Objects_From_Bone(int) overload (slot 35, 0x941F90; VC6 lists overloads in reverse declaration order).
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: Renegade's four user-lighting functions are not virtual (Zero Hour removed them; the client's vtable has no slots for them); kept as plain functions so Renegade's mesh and renderer code still compiles.
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: Set_Has_User_Lighting not virtual.
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: Save_User_Lighting not virtual.
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: Load_User_Lighting not virtual.
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: Zero Hour's IS_SELF_SHADOWED bit: ShdMeshClass::Render (0xA09430) sets and clears 0x80000 in Bits (+0x18).
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: Zero Hour's self-shadow accessors, used by ShdMeshClass::Render (0xA09430).
* `ww3d2/rendobj.h`: E&B's RenderObjClass has Zero Hour's IGNORE_LOD_COST bit (0x8000) and accessors: SimpleSceneClass::Visibility_Check 0x965F40 skips Prepare_LOD when Bits (+0x18) bit 15 is set.

* `wwaudio/wwaudio.cpp`: WWAudioClass::Get_2D_Sample 0x9F1360: E&B limits the sounds of one kind (m_Unknown80) holding handles to m_Unknown7C: it counts them and their lowest runtime priority while scanning every handle (the scan no longer stops at the first free one), and at the limit steals that sound's handle (or gives none). * `ww3d2/sortingrenderer.cpp`: The stream loop's int i (Zero Hour's) is reused by the texture loop, as in Zero Hour; VC6 leaks for-loop variables, so a second declaration does not compile.
* `ww3d2/sortingrenderer.cpp`: E&B's sorting Release_Refs releases textures up to the device's texture stages (Zero Hour's): 0x983300 loops to CurrentCaps (0xC006A0) +0x124, Get_Max_Textures_Per_Pass.
* `ww3d2/sortingrenderer.cpp`: E&B's sorting Apply_Render_State sets textures up to the device's texture stages (Zero Hour's loop; lights and transforms stay in Renegade's order): 0x983BF0 compares the stage with CurrentCaps (0xC006A0) +0x124.
* `ww3d2/dx8vertexbuffer.h`: Zero Hour's 06/26/02 vertex size for non-FVF buffers: the client's DX8VertexBufferClass(FVF, count, usage, vertex_size) at 0x94F330 (ret 0x10) builds new FVFInfoClass(FVF, vertex_size) (0x9BA5D0); ShdDX8RendererNodeClass's constructor (0xA084BE) calls it with FVF 0. Without Zero Hour's W3DMPO base and W3DNEW.

* `ww3d2/dx8renderer.h`: dx8renderer keeps 2 texture stages through MeshMatDescClass::MAX_TEX_STAGES, as Zero Hour changed it when MAX_TEXTURE_STAGES became 8 (its own copy's comment); with MAX_TEXTURE_STAGES there, 11 dx8renderer functions stop matching.
* `ww3d2/dx8renderer.h`: dx8renderer keeps 2 texture stages through MeshMatDescClass::MAX_TEX_STAGES, as Zero Hour changed it when MAX_TEXTURE_STAGES became 8 (its own copy's comment); with MAX_TEXTURE_STAGES there, 11 dx8renderer functions stop matching.

* `wlib/xtime.cpp`: with wstypes.h on WWLib's bittype.h (sint32 long) getYDay's and getTime's `int year` no longer binds to `sint32 &` (C2664); the year is an int as getTime's own parameter. Same code: none of the three matches the client yet.
* `wlib/wstypes.h`: E&B's udpconn.cpp (0x82C5E0-0x82E020) uses wlib's CritSec (0x8B4330 / 0x8B4350) and inlines wlib's Dictionary in one file with WWLib's WWProfile (0x444160), and CordCon inlines wlib's ArrayList beside WWLib's pools, so the two sets of typedefs met in one file (matchbot's char / int / unsigned int redefine bittype.h's: C2371); wlib takes WWLib's, so that sint32 means one type in wlib's objects and the app's (their mangled names agree).
* `wlib/wstypes.h`: as sint8: bittype.h's sint32 / uint32.
* `wnet/tcpmgr.h`: E&B's addition 0x8BD940 (after getIncomingConnection 0x8BD910): finds the pending connection whose handle (+0x10 of its 0x24-byte record) is the argument, closes its socket and removes it (ArrayList remove inlined); tcpconn.cpp's destructor 0x828EC0 and Disconnect 0x829960 call it with their handle (+0x28).
* `wnet/tcpmgr.h`: E&B's STATUS has a -16 (CONNRESET): getStatus 0x8BDD40 returns it for WSAECONNRESET (0x2746) and WSAENOTCONN (0x2749); TCPCon's read 0x8BC280, normalWrite 0x8BC170 and pumpReads 0x8BC4F0 end quietly on it, isConnected 0x8BC8C0 treats it as closed.
* `wnet/tcpmgr.h`: wnet is built with /GX (TCPMgr::wait 0x8BDA80 has an EH frame for its Wtime locals), yet E&B's ~TCPMgr 0x8BCA90 has none: the three ArrayList members' destructors inlined without unwind states. VC6 /GX gives exactly that for a destructor declared throw() (without it: an EH frame with states 1, 0, -1). The form is the compiler's evidence, not a source line.
* `wlib/arraylist.h`: E&B's ArrayList grows by a count: growVector(howmany) makes room for Entries_+howmany ((Entries_+howmany)*2 slots); TCPMgr's ArrayList<PendingConn>::growVector 0x8BE7A0 is out of line with the argument (`add eax, [esp+8]; cmp eax, Slots_`, `ret 4`, its callers in 0x8BD240 push 1), and add's two inlined calls test Entries_+1 (`inc eax`, 0x8BF620, TCPQueue::add 0x8BF1C0).
* `wlib/arraylist.h`: E&B's ArrayList grows by a count: growVector(howmany) makes room for Entries_+howmany ((Entries_+howmany)*2 slots); TCPMgr's ArrayList<PendingConn>::growVector 0x8BE7A0 is out of line with the argument (`add eax, [esp+8]; cmp eax, Slots_`, `ret 4`, its callers in 0x8BD240 push 1), and add's two inlined calls test Entries_+1 (`inc eax`, 0x8BF620, TCPQueue::add 0x8BF1C0).
* `wlib/wtime.cpp`: E&B's msleep 0x8B8270 (between Wtime::Update 0x8B8220 and ParseDate 0x8B8330): select(0, 0, 0, 0, {msec / 1000, msec % 1000 * 1000}); TCPCon's destructor, close and setBufferedWrites call it with 1 (0x8BBCF1, 0x8BBE72, 0x8BBFDA); matchbot's matcher.cpp calls msleep(1), EA's copy has no definition.
* `wlib/wtime.h`: msleep's declaration (0x8B8270, wtime.cpp).
* `wnet/tcpmgr.cpp`: getStatus 0x8BDD40: WSAECONNRESET and WSAENOTCONN give CONNRESET (-16), after WSAEBADF.
* `wnet/tcpmgr.cpp`: wnet is built with /GX (TCPMgr::wait 0x8BDA80 has an EH frame for its Wtime locals), yet E&B's ~TCPMgr 0x8BCA90 has none: the three ArrayList members' destructors inlined without unwind states. VC6 /GX gives exactly that for a destructor declared throw() (without it: an EH frame with states 1, 0, -1). The form is the compiler's evidence, not a source line.
* `binkmovie/binkmovie.cpp`: E&B's WW3D2 is Zero Hour's here: MipCountType is a file-scope enum in texture.h (not TextureClass's).
* `binkmovie/binkmovie.cpp`: E&B's WW3D2 is Zero Hour's here: TextureClass::Peek_D3D_Texture (texture.h), Renegade's Peek_DX8_Texture.

E&B's TCPCon (0x8BBA70-0x8BCA50) on the plan of Renegade's RenegadeGR tcpcon.cpp, written from the client: an OutputDevice over two TCPQueues, a CritSec taken when Locking_ is set, the peer's address cached, TCPMgr::CONNRESET ending reads and writes; E&B's pumpReads, peekRead / endPeekRead, setLocking and print. All 18 functions byte-exact (RenegadeGR's own matches none).
